- **barry-assessment**: Barry 学员英语听口水平测评 + 卡点诊断,10-17 分钟产出口语/听力/综合三个 CEFR 数字 + 听口卡点定位,写入学员档案 Section 2 + 3。所有学员走统一流程:标化考试问询(参考锚) → 中翻英晋级制(起点 B1,过关升级,失败降级,测口语 CEFR + 末尾发音问) → 听力 5 题阶梯式(测听力 CEFR) + 阶段 a/b 卡点诊断 → 综合定级 → 信心检测 + 写档案。中翻英以"意思讲清楚"为基本过关标准,复杂句式/高级词汇是 plus。新人首跑由 barry-onboarding 调起;老学员主动调用以重新评测。触发词:测评、英语水平测评、诊断、English assessment、CEFR、卡点。 ([InterpreterBarry/barry-english-toolkit](https://github.com/InterpreterBarry/barry-english-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/barry-assessment) · ★29)

- **barry-audit-consistency**: Audit the whole Barry repository for inconsistencies between the migrations, the Edge Function, the app code, CONTEXT.md, the spec, the config files, the guides, the tests, the German and English texts, and the platform variants, and report them with both sides quoted. Read-only unless invoked with --fix, which then applies the high-confidence fixes on the less trusted side and commits each one. Use when asked to find inconsistencies, drift, stale docs, or mismatches across the repository.
